the unremitting efforts and persistence of our employees and each        長並且順利完成上市發行。
of the intermediaries, the Company maintained steady business

Business Review of the Reporting Period                                 報告期業務回顧
We are a leading online investment decision-making solution             我們是中國領先的在線投資決策解決方案提

The following is a summary of the principal terms of the Pre-          以下為經2021年6月1日（「採納日期」）董事

February 20, 2023. The Pre-IPO RSU Scheme is not subject to the        主要條款概要。首次公開發售前受限制股份

a.   Purpose                                                           a.   目的
     The purpose of the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme is to reward the                 首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃的
     grantees for their services and contribution to the success            目的乃為嘉許承授人對本集團的成功作
     of the Group, and to provide incentives to them to further             出服務及貢獻，並為彼等對本集團的進
     contribute to the Group.                                               一步貢獻提供獎勵。


b.   RSU Awards                                                        b.   受限制股份單位獎勵
     An award of RSUs under the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme                          首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃項
     (“Award(s)”) gives the participant (the “RSU Participant”) a           下的受限制股份單位獎勵（「獎勵」）給
     contingent right upon vesting of the Award to obtain either            予參與者（「受限制股份單位參與者」）
     Shares or an equivalent value in cash with reference to the            一項或有權利，在獎勵歸屬時獲取經董
     market value of the Shares underlying such Award on or                 事會參考歸屬日期或前後有關獎勵涉及
     about the date of vesting, as determined by the Board in its           的股份的市值而全權酌情釐定的股份或
     sole discretion.                                                       等值現金。

market. We offer various software to help individual investors          們提供各種軟件幫助個人投資者更好地了解

or decisions. We declare that Shareholders are important to the         股東對董事會及本集團整體而言相當重要，
Board and the Group as a whole, and are committed to providing          並致力於提供多元化及可靠的產品及服務，
diversified and reliable products and services that create value for    透過可持續增長和發展為股東創造價值。
Shareholders through sustainable growth and development.

The Board of Directors of the Company provides guidance on              本公司董事會已載列以下價值觀以就員工之
the conduct and behavior of employees and business activities           操守及行為以及業務活動提供指導，並確保
by setting out the following values and ensuring that these values      該等價值觀融入本公司願景、使命、政策及
are integrated into the Company’s vision, mission, policies and         業務策略：
business strategies:

Customer focus, teamwork, concentration, compliance, embracing          以客戶為中心、團隊協作、專注、本分、擁
changes, innovation, living seriously and working happily               抱變化、創新、認真生活快樂工作

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                            風險管理及內部控制
CONTROL
The Board recognizes its responsibility for risk management             董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制制度及
and internal control systems and reviews their effectiveness.           審查其有效性的責任。該等制度旨在管理而
These systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate,            不是消除無法實現業務目標的風險，並只能
the risk of failure to achieve business objectives and can only         提供合理而非絕對的保證，以防止重大誤報
provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material        或損失。
misstatement or loss.

The Board is fully responsible for assessing and determining            董事會全面負責評估及確定其在實現本公司
the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving   策略目標過程中願意承擔的風險的性質及程
the Company’s strategic objectives and for establishing and             度，並建立及維護適當有效的風險管理及內
maintaining appropriate and effective risk management and internal      部控制機制。
control mechanisms.

The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the management         審核委員會協助董事會領導管理層並監督彼
and overseeing their design, implementation and monitoring of risk      等對風險管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施
management and internal control systems. The Audit Committee            及監測。審核委員會負責檢討我們的財務監
is responsible for reviewing our financial control, internal control    控、內部監控及風險管理制度。本公司設立
and risk management systems. The Company has established an             獨立的內部審核部門直接向審核委員會匯報。
independent internal audit department reporting directly to the
Audit Committee.

ESG MISSION AND VISION                                                 ESG使命及願景
Sustainability is gathering global momentum with the rising            隨著人們對氣候變化、人權及社會公義的問
awareness towards issues related to climate change, human              題日益關注，可持續性成為全球勢頭。我們
rights and social justice. We recognize the increasing importance      明白，ESG管理的重要性日益增加，並高度
of ESG management and have attached great importance to                重視履行我們的ESG責任，並實現其ESG使
fulfilling our ESG responsibilities and achieving its ESG mission.     命。我們致力於在業務發展與ESG成就之間
We are dedicated to striking the perfect balance between business      取得完美平衡。
development and ESG achievement.


RISK MANAGEMENT                                                        風險管理
The Group has devoted significant resources to developing our          本集團投入了並將繼續投入大量資源以制定
risk management system and procedures and will continue to do          我們的風險管理制度及程序。我們致力建立
so. We have devoted ourselves to establishing and maintaining          與維護由我們認為適合本身業務運營的政策
risk management and internal control systems consisting of             和程序所組成的風險管理及內部控制系統，
policies and procedures that we consider to be appropriate for         例如人工智能監督管理、財務報告風險管理
our business operations, such as AI supervising management,            和內部控制風險管理，包括關聯方交易風險
financial reporting risk management and internal control risk          管理、數據及內部控制風險管理、數據及信
management, including related party transaction risk management,       息安全管理以及僱員資質及行為合規風險管
data and internal control risk management, data and information        理等。我們定期評估風險管理及內部控制系
security management, and employees’ qualification and behavior         統的運行情況，並根據缺陷的影響將評估中
compliance risk management. We evaluate the operation of our risk      發現的潛在缺陷分為高、中、低三個級別，
management and internal control systems routinely, and categorize      並據此確定整改時間。我們持續改進我們的
the potential defects identified in the evaluation into high, medium   風險管理及內部控制系統，並在進行業務時
and low levels, based on the impact of the defects, and determine      遵照已建立的內部控制系統。
the rectification time accordingly. We continuously improve our
risk management and internal control systems and conduct our
business in compliance with our established internal control
systems.

MANAGING ENVIRONMENTAL FOOTPRINT                                       管理環境足跡
Pollution Control and Greenhouse Gases                                 污染防治與溫室氣體
Air emissions                                                          廢氣排放
The Group’s business operations do not involve in industrial           本集團的業務運營不涉及工業生產及製造領
production and manufacturing areas, therefore air emissions            域，故報告期內廢氣排放僅來源於公司所
during the Reporting Period were only from exhaust emissions           擁有車輛行駛過程的尾氣排放，而不涉及氣
by the self-owned vehicles of the Company in the process of            體及固體燃料消耗所產生的廢氣排放。本集
running and did not involve in air emissions from gas and solid fuel   團提倡僱員在工作過程中，盡量使用公共交
consumption. The Group encourages its employees to take public         通，以減少汽車尾氣的排放，故在報告期
transport means as much as possible in the course of their work        內，相比於同僱員規模之企業，本集團廢氣
for the purpose of reducing vehicle exhaust emissions. Therefore,      排放密度相對較低。
during the Reporting Period, the Group’s air emission intensity was
relatively low as compared to other enterprises with the same size
of employees.

The Group considered that the use of vehicles in our business          本集團認為我們在業務運營中的車輛使用極
operation was minimal and the relevant exhaust emissions and           少，且與其他ESG問題相比，公司車輛產生
direct greenhouse gas emissions produced from the Company’s            的相關廢氣排放及直接溫室氣體排放對其業
vehicles were less material to its business and stakeholders           務及持份者的重要性較低，因此並無計劃就
when comparing to other ESG issues, thus it has no plans to set        廢氣排放設定數字性排放目標。若業務模式
quantitative emission target for exhaust emissions. If there is a      隨後發生變化，導致公司擁有車輛所產生之
subsequent change in the business model which involves significant     廢氣排放大幅增加，本集團將進一步評估設
increase in exhaust emissions generated from the vehicles owned        定數據性目標的必要性。儘管如此，本集團
by the Company, the Group will further evaluate the need to set        依然設定方向性目標，即在未來五年盡量減
quantitative target. Nevertheless, the Group has set a directional     少廢氣排放並優化減排措施，有關詳情將於
target, that is, to minimize exhaust emissions and optimize emission   下文「管理環境足跡－環境及天然資源」一節
reduction measures over the next five years, details of which are      做出說明。
described in the section “Managing Environmental Footprint –
Environment and Natural Resources” below.

personal information. This will help to ensure that no child labor is   為避免強迫勞動，本公司制定公平的僱員工
recruited at source. In addition, to avoid forced labor, the Company    作時間表，鼓勵僱員在工作時間內完成本

     not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is           謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述或淩駕
     higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve          於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐
     collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations           而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能
     or the override of internal control.                                    發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風
                                                                             險。


•    Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the         •   了解與審核相關的內部控制，以於有關
     audit in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate          情況下設計適當的審核程序，但目的並
     in the circumstances but not for the purpose of expressing an           非對 貴集團內部控制的有效性發表意
     opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.           見。


•    Evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used            •   評估董事所採用會計政策的恰當性及董
     and the reasonableness of accounting estimates and related              事所作出會計估計及相關披露的合理
     disclosures made by the directors.                                      性。


•    Conclude on the appropriateness of the directors’ use of the        •   對董事採用持續經營會計基礎的恰當性
     going concern basis of accounting and, based on the audit               作出結論。根據所獲取的審計憑證，確
     evidence obtained, whether a material uncertainty exists                定是否存在與事項或情況有關的重大不
     related to events or conditions that may cast significant doubt         確定性，從而可能導致對 貴集團的持
     on the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern. If we            續經營能力產生重大疑慮。倘我們認為

          Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations issued by the                  報告準則、香港會計準則（「香港

          (“HKICPA”), accounting principles generally accepted                   認會計原則和香港公司條例之要
          in Hong Kong and the requirements of the Hong Kong                     求。該等財務報表亦遵守香港聯
          Companies Ordinance. These financial statements also                   合交易所有限公司證券上市規則

          Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock                 的重要會計政策披露如下。
          Exchange of Hong Kong Limited. Significant accounting
          policies adopted by the group are disclosed below.

          The HKICPA has issued certain amendments to                            香港會計師公會已頒佈對香港財
          HKFRSs that are first effective or available for early                 務報告準則的若干修訂，該等修

          Note 1(c) provides information on any changes in                       效或可供提早採用。由於初步應

    (b)   Basis of preparation of the financial statements                 (b)   財務報表之編製基準
          JF Wealth Holdings Ltd (the “Company ”), was                           九 方 財 富 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公
          incorporated on 3 May 2021 in the Cayman Islands as                    司」）於2021 年5 月3 日根據開曼
          an exempted company with limited liability under the                   群島法律第22章公司法（1961年
          Companies Act, Cap.22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated                  第3號法律，經綜合及修訂）在開
          and revised) of the Cayman Islands.                                    曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公
                                                                                 司。

          The Company is an investment holding company                           本公司為投資控股公司，除下述
          and has not carried on any business since the date                     集團重組外，自註冊成立日期起

    (h)   Leased assets                                                       (h)   租賃資產
          At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                    於合約開始時，本集團評估合約
          the contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or                  是否屬於或包含租賃。倘合約賦
          contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to                    予已識別資產在一段期間內的使
          control the use of an identified asset for a period of                    用控制權以換取代價，則該合約
          time in exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed                   屬於或包含租賃。倘客戶既有權
          where the customer has both the right to direct the use                   主導已識別資產的使用亦有權從
          of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of                有關使用中獲取絕大部分經濟利
          the economic benefits from that use.                                      益，即賦予控制權。

          Where the contract contains lease component(s) and                        倘合約包含租賃部分及非租賃部
          non-lease component(s), the Group has elected not                         分，本集團已選擇不區分非租賃
          to separate non-lease components and accounts for                         部分，並就所有租賃將各租賃部
          each lease component and any associated non-lease                         分及任何相關非租賃部分以單一
          components as a single lease component for all leases.                    租賃部分入賬。

          At the lease commencement date, the Group recognises                      於租賃開始日期，本集團確認使
          a right-of-use asset and a lease liability, except for short-             用權資產及租賃負債，惟租期為
          term leases that have a lease term of 12 months or less                   12個月或以下的短期租賃及低價
          and leases of low-value assets. When the Group enters                     值資產租賃除外。當本集團就低
          into a lease in respect of a low-value asset, the Group                   價值資產訂立租賃時，本集團就
          decides whether to capitalise the lease on a lease-by-                    每份租賃決定是否進行資本化。
          lease basis. The lease payments associated with those                     未資本化租賃相關的租賃付款於
          leases which are not capitalised are recognised as an                     租期內有系統地確認為開支。
          expense on a systematic basis over the lease term.

          Where the lease is capitalised, the lease liability is                    倘租賃資本化，租賃負債初步按
          initially recognised at the present value of the lease                    租期內應付的租賃付款現值確
          payments payable over the lease term, discounted                          認，並使用租賃隱含的利率（或
          using the interest rate implicit in the lease or, if that                 倘該利率無法釐定，則使用相關
          rate cannot be readily determined, using a relevant                       增量借款利率）貼現。初步確認
          incremental borrowing rate. After initial recognition,                    後，租賃負債按攤銷成本計量，
          the lease liability is measured at amortised cost and                     利息開支則使用實際利率法計
          interest expense is calculated using the effective interest               算。不取決於指數或利率的可變
          method. Variable lease payments that do not depend on                     租賃付款並不計入租賃負債的計
          an index or rate are not included in the measurement of                   量，故於其產生的會計期間自損
          the lease liability and hence are charged to profit or loss               益扣除。
          in the accounting period in which they are incurred.

2   ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT AND                                           2   會計判斷及估計
    ESTIMATES
    In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,                  應用本集團會計政策的過程中，
    management has made the following accounting judgements:                     管理層已作出以下會計判斷：

    (a)   Recognition of income taxes and deferred tax                     (a)   確認所得稅及遞延稅項資產
          assets
          Determining income tax provision involves judgement                    釐定所得稅撥備涉及對若干交易
          on the future tax treatment of certain transactions.                   的未來稅項處理的判斷。管理層
          Management evaluates tax implications of transactions                  評估交易的稅務影響並相應劃撥
          and tax provisions are set up accordingly. The tax                     稅項撥備。有關交易的稅務處理
          treatments of such transactions are reconsidered                       會定期重新檢討，並計及所有稅
          periodically to take into account all changes in tax                   法的變動。遞延稅項資產乃就可
          legislation. Deferred tax assets are recognised in respect             抵扣暫時性差異確認。由於該等
          of deductible temporary differences. As those deferred                 遞延稅項資產只能在可能有未來
          tax assets can only be recognised to the extent that it                應課稅溢利用於抵扣可抵扣暫時
          is probable that future taxable profits will be available              性差異的情況下才能確認，因此
          against which the deductible temporary differences                     需要管理層判斷以評估未來應課
          can be utilised, management’s judgement is required                    稅溢利的可能性。倘未來有可能
          to assess the probability of future taxable profits.                   產生應課稅溢利從而收回遞延稅
          Management’s assessment is revised as necessary                        項資產，管理層的評估於必要時
          and additional deferred tax assets are recognised if it                修訂及額外遞延稅項資產會予以
          becomes probable that future taxable profits will allow                確認。
          the deferred tax asset to be recovered.

 22 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                                         22 財務風險管理及金融工具的
    VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                               公平值
        Exposure to credit risk, liquidity risk, interest rate risk, foreign     面對的信貸風險、流動性風險、利率風
        exchange risk and price risk arises in the normal course of the          險、外匯風險及價格風險於本集團的一
        Group’s business. The Group’s exposure to these risks and                般業務過程中出現。本集團面臨該等風
        the financial risk management policies and practice used by              險及本集團管理該等風險所用的財務風
        the Group to manage these risks are described below.                     險管理政策及常規載列如下。

        (a)    Credit risk                                                       (a)   信貸風險
               Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will                 信貸風險指對手方未履行其合約
               default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                   責任導致本集團蒙受財務損失
               financial loss to the Group. The Group did not have                     的風險。本集團並無貿易應收款
               trade receivables. The counterparties of the Group’s                    項。本集團其他應收款項的對手
               other receivables are mainly the Group’s related parties,               方主要為本集團的關聯方，而本
               for which the Group considers to have low credit risk.                  集團認為其信貸風險較低。面對
               The exposure to credit risk arising from cash and cash                  的現金及現金等價物所產生信貸
               equivalents is limited because the counterparties are                   風險有限，因為對手方為擁有最
               banks and financial institutions with a minimum credit                  低信貸風險的銀行及金融機構。
               risk.

               The Group does not provide any guarantees which                         本集團概無提供將使本集團面臨
               would expose the Group to credit risk.                                  信貸風險的任何擔保。

        (b)    Liquidity risk                                                    (b)   流動性風險
               Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will not be able              流動性風險指本集團將不能履行
               to meet its financial obligations as they fall due. The                 其到期財務責任的風險。本集團
               Group’s approach to managing liquidity is to ensure, as                 管理流動性的方針，是盡可能保
               far as possible, that it will have sufficient cash to meet              證其在正常或受壓情況下備有充
               its liabilities when they are due, under both normal and                足現金，以償還到期負債，而不
               stressed conditions, without incurring unacceptable                     會產生過高損失或引致本集團聲
               losses or risking damage to the Group’s reputation.                     譽受損。

               The Group’s policy is to regularly monitor current and                  本集團的政策是定期監察現時及
               expected liquidity requirements, and to ensure that it                  預期流動資金需求，並確保其備
               maintains sufficient reserves of cash to meet its liquidity             有充足現金儲備，以滿足長短期
               requirements in the short and longer term.                              流動資金需求。

               The following tables show the remaining contractual                     下表列示於2021 年及2022 年年

               undiscounted cash flows (including interest payments                    告期末當時利率計算的利息款
               computed using contractual rates or, if floating, based                 項）計算本集團金融負債的合約
               on rates current at the end of the reporting period) and                到期日剩餘期間以及本集團或須

               is not exposed to significant interest rate risk during the            團於2021 年及2022 年並無面臨
               years of 2021 and 2022.                                                重大利率風險。

               other than the respective functional currencies of the                 相關功能貨幣以外的貨幣計值。
               Group’s entities.

               the price fluctuation of the financial instruments at fair             金融工具的價格波動所引致純利
               value through profit or loss.                                          及股本按比例波動。

               The sensitivity analysis indicates the instantaneous                   敏感度分析顯示假設股票市場指
               change in the Group’s net profit and equity that would                 數或其他相關風險變數於報告期

               to re-measure those financial instruments held by the                  引致本集團面臨價格風險的金融
               Group which expose the Group to price risk at the                      工具。此項亦假設本集團股本證
